\u5ef6\u8fdf\u8ffd\u8e2a\u5de5\u5177\u94fe\u7684\u751f\u4ea7\u914d\u7f6e\n\n**OpenTelemetry RAG Pipeline\u63d2\u6869\uff1a**\n\n```python\nfrom opentelemetry import trace\nfrom opentelemetry.sdk.trace import TracerProvider\nfrom opentelemetry.sdk.trace.export import BatchSpanProcessor\nfrom opentelemetry.exporter.otlp.proto.grpc.trace_exporter import OTLPSpanExporter\n\ntrace.set_tracer_provider(TracerProvider())\ntracer = trace.get_tracer(\"rag_pipeline\")\notlp_exporter = OTLPSpanExporter(endpoint=\"localhost:4317\", insecure=True)\ntrace.get_tracer_provider().add_span_processor(BatchSpanProcessor(otlp_exporter))\n\ndef rag_query(query: str):\n    with tracer.start_as_current_span(\"rag_pipeline\") as span:\n        span.set_attribute(\"query.length\", len(query))\n        \n        with tracer.start_as_current_span(\"embedding_generation\"):\n            embedding = generate_embedding(query)\n        \n        with tracer.start_as_current_span(\"vector_search\") as search_span:\n            results = vector_db.search(embedding, k=20)\n            search_span.set_attribute(\"results.count\", len(results))\n        \n        with tracer.start_as_current_span(\"reranking\"):\n            reranked = reranker.rerank(query, results)\n        \n        with tracer.start_as_current_span(\"llm_generation\"):\n            response = llm.generate(query, context=reranked)\n        \n        return response\n```\n\n**py-spy\u706b\u7130\u56fe\u5206\u6790\uff08\u751f\u4ea7\u5b89\u5168\uff09\uff1a**\n```bash\n# \u5b9e\u65f6Top\u89c6\u56fe\n```\n\n**Pyinstrument\u7edf\u8ba1\u91c7\u6837\uff1a** \u6bcf1ms\u91c7\u6837\u4e00\u6b21\uff0c\u6d4b\u91cf\u5899\u949f\u65f6\u95f4\uff08\u5305\u62ecI/O\u7b49\u5f85\uff09\uff0c\u7279\u522b\u9002\u5408\u5305\u542b\u7f51\u7edc\u8c03\u7528\u7684RAG\u7ba1\u9053\u5206\u6790\u3002\n\n### \u591a\u7ea7\u7f13\u5b58\u7b56\u7565\u5b9e\u73b0\u8bed\u4e49\u7ea7\u547d\u4e2d\n\n\u7f13\u5b58\u662fRAG\u6027\u80fd\u4f18\u5316\u7684\u6838\u5fc3\u6760\u6746\u3002\u7814\u7a76\u8868\u660e**31%\u7684LLM\u67e5\u8be2\u5177\u6709\u4e0a\u4e0b\u6587\u53ef\u91cd\u590d\u6027**\uff0c\u8bed\u4e49\u7f13\u5b58\u53ef\u663e\u8457\u63d0\u5347\u547d\u4e2d\u7387\u3002\n\n**RedisVL\u8bed\u4e49\u7f13\u5b58\u914d\u7f6e\uff1a**\n\n```python\nfrom redisvl.extensions.cache.llm import SemanticCache\n\nllmcache = SemanticCache(\n    name=\"llmcache\",\n    ttl=360,\n    redis_url=\"redis://localhost:6379\",\n    distance_threshold=0.1  # \u4f59\u5f26\u8ddd\u79bb\u9608\u503c\uff0c\u8d8a\u5c0f\u8d8a\u4e25\u683c\n)\n\ndef cached_llm_call(query: str, llm):\n    cached = llmcache.check(prompt=query)\n    if cached:\n        return cached[0]['response']\n    \n    response = llm.generate(query)\n    llmcache.store(prompt=query, response=response)\n    return response\n```\n\n**\u4e24\u7ea7Embedding\u7f13\u5b58\u6a21\u5f0f\uff1a**\n\n```python\nfrom functools import lru_cache\nimport hashlib\n\nclass EmbeddingService:\n    def __init__(self, model, cache_size=10000):\n        self.model = model\n        self._cache = {}\n    \n    def embed_with_redis_fallback(self, text: str, redis_client):\n        cache_key = hashlib.md5(text.encode()).hexdigest()\n        \n        # L1: \u5185\u5b58\u7f13\u5b58\uff08<1ms\uff09\n        if cache_key in self._cache:\n            return self._cache[cache_key]\n        \n        # L2: Redis\u7f13\u5b58\uff081-5ms\uff09\n        cached = redis_client.get(f\"emb:{cache_key}\")\n        if cached:\n            embedding = json.loads(cached)\n            self._cache[cache_key] = embedding\n            return embedding\n        \n        # \u8ba1\u7b97\u5e76\u7f13\u5b58\n        embedding = self.model.encode(text).tolist()\n        redis_client.setex(f\"emb:{cache_key}\", 3600, json.dumps(embedding))\n        self._cache[cache_key] = embedding\n        return embedding\n```\n\n**TTL\u7b56\u7565\u63a8\u8350\uff1a**\n| \u7f13\u5b58\u7c7b\u578b | \u63a8\u8350TTL | \u7406\u7531 |\n|---------|--------|-----|\n| Query Cache | 1-24\u5c0f\u65f6 | \u5b8c\u5168\u5339\u914d\u67e5\u8be2\u53d8\u5316\u5c11 |\n| Embedding Cache | 24-168\u5c0f\u65f6 | \u5d4c\u5165\u5411\u91cf\u7a33\u5b9a\uff08\u9664\u975e\u6a21\u578b\u66f4\u6362\uff09 |\n| Semantic Cache | 30\u5206\u949f-6\u5c0f\u65f6 | \u9884\u8ba1\u7b97\u4e0e\u589e\u91cf\u7d22\u5f15\u7684\u751f\u4ea7\u5b9e\u8df5\n\n**\u5206\u5757\u7b56\u7565\u6700\u4f73\u5b9e\u8df5\uff08Chroma 2024\u6280\u672f\u62a5\u544a\uff09\uff1a**\n- **200 2024\uff09\uff1a** \u901a\u8fc7\u4e3a\u6bcf\u4e2a\u5206\u5757\u6dfb\u52a0\u4e0a\u4e0b\u6587\u63cf\u8ff0\uff0c\u68c0\u7d22\u9519\u8bef\u964d\u4f4e\u9ad8\u8fbe**67%**\uff08\u914d\u5408\u91cd\u6392\u5e8f\uff09\u3002\n\n```python\nCONTEXTUAL_PROMPT = \"\"\"<document>\n{WHOLE_DOCUMENT}\n</document>\n<chunk>\n{CHUNK_CONTENT}\n</chunk>\n\u8bf7\u63d0\u4f9b\u7b80\u77ed\u4e0a\u4e0b\u6587\uff0c\u4ee5\u4fbf\u66f4\u597d\u5730\u68c0\u7d22\u6b64\u5206\u5757\u3002\u4ec5\u8f93\u51fa\u4e0a\u4e0b\u6587\uff0c\u4e0d\u8981\u5176\u4ed6\u5185\u5bb9\u3002\"\"\"\n\nasync def situate_context(doc: str, chunk: str) -> str:\n    response = await client.messages.create(\n        model=\"claude-3-haiku-20240307\",\n        max_tokens=150,\n        messages=[{\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": CONTEXTUAL_PROMPT.format(\n            WHOLE_DOCUMENT=doc, CHUNK_CONTENT=chunk\n        )}]\n    )\n    return response.content[0].text\n```\n\n**CDC\u96c6\u6210\u67b6\u6784\uff08Debezium + Kafka\uff09\uff1a**\n\n```\nPostgreSQL(WAL) \u2192 Debezium \u2192 Kafka \u2192 CDC Consumer + Embedder \u2192 Vector DB\n```\n\n```python\nfrom kafka import KafkaConsumer\nimport json\n\nconsumer = KafkaConsumer(\n    'rag.documents',\n    bootstrap_servers=['kafka:9092'],\n    value_deserializer=lambda m: json.loads(m.decode('utf-8'))\n)\n\nasync def process_cdc_events():\n    for message in consumer:\n        event = message.value\n        if event['op'] in ['c', 'u']:  # create\u6216update\n            embedding = await embed_document(event['after']['content'])\n            await vector_db.upsert(\n                id=event['after']['id'],\n                vector=embedding,\n                metadata=event['after']\n            )\n        elif event['op'] == 'd':  # delete\n            await vector_db.delete(event['before']['id'])\n```\n\n### \u5f02\u6b65\u68c0\u7d22\u4e0e\u591a\u6e90\u5e76\u884c\u53ec\u56de\n\n**\u5b8c\u6574\u7684\u5f02\u6b65\u6df7\u5408\u68c0\u7d22\u5b9e\u73b0\uff1a**\n\n```python\nimport asyncio\nfrom typing import List, Dict\n\nclass AsyncHybridRetriever:\n    def __init__(self, vector_store, bm25_retriever, reranker):\n        self.vector_store = vector_store\n        self.bm25_retriever = bm25_retriever\n        self.reranker = reranker\n    \n    async def aretrieve(self, query: str, top_k: int = 10) -> List[Dict]:\n        # \u5e76\u884c\u6267\u884c\u8bed\u4e49\u548c\u5173\u952e\u8bcd\u68c0\u7d22\n        semantic_task = self.vector_store.asimilarity_search(query, k=top_k)\n        keyword_task = asyncio.to_thread(\n            self.bm25_retriever.get_relevant_documents, query\n        )\n        \n        semantic_docs, keyword_docs = await asyncio.gather(\n            semantic_task, keyword_task\n        )\n        \n        # RRF\u878d\u5408\n        fused_docs = self.reciprocal_rank_fusion(\n            [semantic_docs, keyword_docs], k=60\n        )\n        \n        # Cross-encoder\u91cd\u6392\u5e8f\n        reranked = await asyncio.to_thread(\n            self.reranker.rerank, query, fused_docs[:20]\n        )\n        \n        return reranked[:top_k]\n    \n    def reciprocal_rank_fusion(self, result_lists: List[List], k: int = 60) -> List:\n        \"\"\"RRF\u516c\u5f0f: score = \u03a3 1/(k + rank)\"\"\"\n        scores = {}\n        for results in result_lists:\n            for rank, doc in enumerate(results):\n                doc_id = doc.page_content\n                if doc_id not in scores:\n                    scores[doc_id] = {'doc': doc, 'score': 0}\n                scores[doc_id]['score'] += 1 / (k + rank + 1)\n        \n        sorted_docs = sorted(scores.values(), key=lambda x: x['score'], reverse=True)\n        return [item['doc'] for item in sorted_docs]\n```\n\n**RRF\u7684\u751f\u4ea7\u4f18\u52bf\uff08Assembled.com\u7ecf\u9a8c\uff09\uff1a**\n- \u7b80\u5355\u6027\uff1a\u6bd4\u590d\u6742\u65b9\u6cd5\u66f4\u4e0d\u6613\u8fc7\u62df\u5408\n- \u65e0\u9700\u5f52\u4e00\u5316\uff1a\u57fa\u4e8e\u6392\u540d\u4f4d\u7f6e\u800c\u975e\u5206\u6570\n- \u6700\u5c0f\u8c03\u53c2\uff1ak=60\u5728\u591a\u6837\u6570\u636e\u96c6\u4e0a\u8868\u73b0\u826f\u597d\n\n**\u8d85\u65f6\u63a7\u5236\u4e0e\u7194\u65ad\u964d\u7ea7\uff1a**\n\n```python\nclass CircuitBreaker:\n    def __init__(self, failure_threshold: int = 5, reset_timeout: int = 30):\n        self.failures = 0\n        self.threshold = failure_threshold\n        self.is_open = False\n    \n    async def call(self, coro, timeout: float = 5.0, fallback=None):\n        if self.is_open:\n            return fallback\n        \n        try:\n            result = await asyncio.wait_for(coro, timeout=timeout)\n            self.failures = 0\n            return result\n        except asyncio.TimeoutError:\n            self.failures += 1\n            if self.failures >= self.threshold:\n                self.is_open = True\n                asyncio.create_task(self._reset_after(self.reset_timeout))\n            return fallback\n```\n\n### P99 < 200ms\u76ee\u6807\u7684\u5b9e\u73b0\u8def\u5f84\n\n**ElevenLabs\u751f\u4ea7\u6848\u4f8b\uff1a** \u67e5\u8be2\u6539\u5199\u5360RAG\u5ef6\u8fdf**80%+**\uff0c\u901a\u8fc7\u6a21\u578b\u7ade\u901f\u67b6\u6784\u89e3\u51b3\u3002\n\n```python\nasync def model_racing_query_rewrite(\n    query: str, history: list, timeout: float = 1.0\n) -> str:\n    tasks = [\n        asyncio.create_task(call_model(qwen_4b_rewrite, \"qwen-4b\")),\n        asyncio.create_task(call_model(qwen_30b_rewrite, \"qwen-30b\")),\n        asyncio.create_task(call_model(openai_rewrite, \"openai\")),\n    ]\n    \n    done, pending = await asyncio.wait(\n        tasks, timeout=timeout, return_when=asyncio.FIRST_COMPLETED\n    )\n    \n    for task in pending:\n        task.cancel()\n    \n    for task in done:\n        result = task.result()\n        if result and \u6a21\u578b\u9009\u62e9\u7b56\u7565\u5b9e\u73b0\u667a\u80fd\u8def\u7531\n\n**Semantic Router\u5b9e\u73b0\uff08Aurelio Labs\uff09\uff1a**\n\n```python\nfrom semantic_router import Route\nfrom \u8d28\u91cf-\u6210\u672c\u5e15\u7d2f\u6258\u524d\u6cbf\u4f18\u5316\n\n**RAGAS\u8d28\u91cf\u6307\u6807\u5b9e\u73b0\uff1a**\n\n```python\nfrom ragas import evaluate\nfrom ragas.metrics import \u57fa\u51c6\u6d4b\u8bd5\u6846\u67b6\u8bbe\u8ba1\u4e0e\u9009\u578b\n\n**BEIR\uff08Benchmarking IR\uff09\uff1a** NeurIPS 2021\u5f15\u5165\uff0cSIGIR 2024\u66f4\u65b0\u300218\u4e2a\u6570\u636e\u96c6\u8986\u76d69\u79cd\u68c0\u7d22\u4efb\u52a1\uff1a\u95ee\u7b54\uff08NQ\u3001HotpotQA\uff09\u3001\u4e8b\u5b9e\u6838\u67e5\uff08FEVER\uff09\u3001\u751f\u7269\u533b\u5b66\uff08BioASQ\uff09\u3001\u79d1\u5b66\uff08SciFact\uff09\u7b49\u3002\n\n```python\nfrom beir import util\nfrom beir.retrieval import models\nfrom beir.datasets.data_loader import GenericDataLoader\nfrom beir.retrieval.evaluation import EvaluateRetrieval\nfrom beir.retrieval.search.dense import DenseRetrievalExactSearch as DRES\n\ndataset = \"scifact\"\nurl = f\"https://public.ukp.informatik.tu-darmstadt.de/thakur/BEIR/datasets/{dataset}.zip\"\ndata_path = util.download_and_unzip(url, \"datasets\")\ncorpus, queries, qrels = GenericDataLoader(data_folder=data_path).load(split=\"test\")\n\nmodel = DRES(models.SentenceBERT(\"sentence-transformers/all-MiniLM-L6-v2\"), batch_size=128)\nretriever = EvaluateRetrieval(model, score_function=\"cos_sim\")\nresults = retriever.retrieve(corpus, queries)\n\nndcg, _map, recall, precision = retriever.evaluate(qrels, results, retriever.k_values)\n```\n\n**MTEB Top\u6a21\u578b\uff082024-2025\uff09\uff1a**\n| \u6a21\u578b | MTEB\u5206\u6570 | \u7279\u70b9 |\n|-----|---------|-----|\n| Qwen3 GTE (8B) | ~70+ | \u957f\u6587\u672c\u7406\u89e3\uff0c\u591a\u8bed\u8a00 |\n| NV-Embed-v2 | 69.32 | \u6f5c\u5728\u6ce8\u610f\u529b\u5c42\uff0c\u5bf9\u6bd4\u5b66\u4e60 |\n| BGE-large-en-v1.5 | 64.23 | \u5f3a\u901a\u7528\u6027 |\n| E5-mistral-7b-instruct | 63.8 | \u6307\u4ee4\u5fae\u8c03 |\n\n**RAGAS 2024-2025\u66f4\u65b0\uff1a** \u81ea\u5b9a\u4e49\u6307\u6807\u88c5\u9970\u5668`@experiment()`\u3001A/B\u6d4b\u8bd5\u6846\u67b6\u96c6\u6210\u3001AspectCritic\u81ea\u5b9a\u4e49\u8bc4\u4f30\u6807\u51c6\u3001\u5feb\u901f\u542f\u52a8\u6a21\u677f\u3002\n\n### \u5ef6\u8fdf/\u541e\u5410\u91cf/\u8d28\u91cf\u4e09\u89d2\u6743\u8861\n\n**\u5ef6\u8fdf\u6307\u6807\u5b9a\u4e49\u4e0e\u76ee\u6807\uff1a**\n\n| \u6307\u6807 | \u5b9a\u4e49 | \u5178\u578b\u76ee\u6807 |\n|-----|-----|---------|\n| P50 | \u4e2d\u4f4d\u5ef6\u8fdf | <500ms\uff08\u804a\u5929\u673a\u5668\u4eba\uff09 |\n| P95 | 95%\u8bf7\u6c42\u5b8c\u6210 | <1000ms |\n| P99 | 99%\u8bf7\u6c42\u5b8c\u6210 | <2000ms |\n| TTFT | \u9996Token\u65f6\u95f4 | <200ms\uff08\u804a\u5929\uff09\uff0c<100ms\uff08\u4ee3\u7801\u8865\u5168\uff09 |\n| ITL | Token\u95f4\u5ef6\u8fdf | <50ms\uff08\u6d41\u7545\u6d41\u5f0f\uff09 |\n\n**\u541e\u5410\u91cf\u6307\u6807\uff1a**\n- **QPS**: \u6bcf\u79d2\u67e5\u8be2\u6570\n- **Tokens/s**: \u8f93\u5165/\u8f93\u51faToken\u541e\u5410\n- **Goodput**: \u6ee1\u8db3SLO\u7684RPS\n\n```\nGoodput (P90 TTFT < 200ms AND P90 TPOT < 50ms) = \n    \u6ee1\u8db3\u4e24\u4e2a\u7ea6\u675f\u768490%\u8bf7\u6c42\u7684\u6700\u5927RPS\n```\n\n**\u8d28\u91cf\u6307\u6807\u5b9e\u73b0\uff1a**\n\n```python\ndef mean_reciprocal_rank(y_true, y_pred):\n    reciprocal_ranks = []\n    for true_docs, pred_docs in zip(y_true, y_pred):\n        for rank, doc in enumerate(pred_docs, start=1):\n            if doc in true_docs:\n                reciprocal_ranks.append(1 / rank)\n                break\n        else:\n            reciprocal_ranks.append(0)\n    return sum(reciprocal_ranks) / len(reciprocal_ranks)\n# \u76ee\u6807: > 0.6\n\ndef ndcg_at_k(relevance_scores, k):\n    import numpy as np\n    dcg = sum(rel / np.log2(idx + 2) for idx, rel in enumerate(relevance_scores[:k]))\n    ideal_scores = sorted(relevance_scores, reverse=True)\n    idcg = sum(rel / np.log2(idx + 2) for idx, rel in enumerate(ideal_scores[:k]))\n    return dcg / idcg if idcg > 0 else 0\n# \u76ee\u6807: > 0.7\uff08\u751f\u4ea7\uff09\n```\n\n**\u51b3\u7b56\u77e9\u9635\uff1a**\n| \u4f18\u5148\u7ea7 | \u4f55\u65f6\u9009\u62e9 | \u793a\u4f8b\u7528\u4f8b |\n|-------|---------|---------|\n| \u8d28\u91cf\u4f18\u5148 | \u9ad8\u98ce\u9669\u51b3\u7b56 | \u533b\u7597\u8bca\u65ad\u3001\u6cd5\u5f8b |\n| \u5ef6\u8fdf\u4f18\u5148 | \u5b9e\u65f6\u4ea4\u4e92 | \u804a\u5929\u3001\u4ee3\u7801\u8865\u5168 |\n| \u541e\u5410\u4f18\u5148 | \u6279\u5904\u7406 | \u6587\u6863\u6458\u8981 |\n| \u5e73\u8861 | \u901a\u7528\u751f\u4ea7 | \u5ba2\u6237\u652f\u6301 |\n\n### A/B\u6d4b\u8bd5\u4e0e\u5728\u7ebf\u8bc4\u4f30\n\n**\u6837\u672c\u91cf\u8ba1\u7b97\uff1a**\n\n```python\nfrom scipy import stats\nimport numpy as np\n\ndef sample_size_for_ab_test(\n    baseline_rate=0.10,\n    minimum_detectable_effect=0.02,\n    alpha=0.05,\n    power=0.80\n):\n    effect_size = minimum_detectable_effect / np.sqrt(baseline_rate * (1 - baseline_rate))\n    analysis = stats.power.TTestIndPower()\n    n = analysis.solve_power(effect_size, power=power, alpha=alpha)\n    return int(np.ceil(n))\n\n# \u793a\u4f8b\uff1a\u6bcf\u7ec4\u7ea63,900\u4e2a\u6837\u672c\n```\n\n**Interleaving\u5b9e\u9a8c\uff1a** \u5c06\u4e24\u4e2a\u7cfb\u7edf\u7684\u7ed3\u679c\u5408\u5e76\u5230\u5355\u4e2a\u5217\u8868\uff0c\u8ffd\u8e2a\u54ea\u4e2a\u7cfb\u7edf\u7684\u7ed3\u679c\u88ab\u70b9\u51fb\u3002\u6bd4\u4f20\u7edfA/B\u6d4b\u8bd5**\u654f\u611f10-100\u500d**\uff0c\u6240\u9700\u6d41\u91cf\u66f4\u5c11\u3002\n\n```python\ndef team_draft_interleave(ranking_a, ranking_b, k=10):\n    interleaved = []\n    a_team, b_team = [], []\n    \n    for i in range(k):\n        team = 'a' if len(a_team) <= len(b_team) else 'b'\n        if team == 'a' and ranking_a:\n            doc = ranking_a.pop(0)\n            a_team.append(doc)\n        elif ranking_b:\n            doc = ranking_b.pop(0)\n            b_team.append(doc)\n        interleaved.append((doc, team))\n    \n    return interleaved, a_team, b_team\n```\n\n**\u5728\u7ebf\u8bc4\u4f30\u6307\u6807\uff1a**\n- \u7528\u6237\u6ee1\u610f\u5ea6\u4ee3\u7406\uff1a\u70b9\u8d5e/\u70b9\u8e29\u3001\u91cd\u65b0\u751f\u6210\u8bf7\u6c42\uff08\u8d1f\u9762\u4fe1\u53f7\uff09\n- \u70b9\u51fb\u7387\uff1a\u5f15\u7528/\u6765\u6e90\u70b9\u51fb\u3001\u540e\u7eed\u95ee\u9898\u7387\n- \u4efb\u52a1\u5b8c\u6210\u7387\uff1a\u67e5\u8be2\u7ec6\u5316\u6b21\u6570\u3001\u653e\u5f03\u7387\u3001\u4efb\u52a1\u5b8c\u6210\u65f6\u95f4\n\n### \u56de\u5f52\u6d4b\u8bd5\u4e0eCI/CD\u96c6\u6210\n\n**DeepEval RAG\u56de\u5f52\u6d4b\u8bd5\u6846\u67b6\uff1a**\n\n```python\nfrom deepeval import assert_test\nfrom deepeval.test_case import [\n        ContextualPrecisionMetric(threshold=0.7),\n        ContextualRecallMetric(threshold=0.7),\n        AnswerRelevancyMetric(threshold=0.8),\n        FaithfulnessMetric(threshold=0.9)\n    ]\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"test_case\", load_golden_dataset())\ndef test_rag_quality(test_case, rag_metrics):\n    response, context = rag_pipeline(test_case['query'])\n    \n    llm_test_case = LLMTestCase(\n        input=test_case['query'],\n        actual_output=response,\n        expected_output=test_case['expected'],\n        retrieval_context=context\n    )\n    \n    assert_test(llm_test_case, rag_metrics)\n```\n\n**GitHub Actions\u914d\u7f6e\uff1a**\n\n```yaml\nname: RAG Regression Tests\non: [push, pull_request]\n\njobs:\n  rag-eval:\n    runs-on: \u751f\u4ea7\u76d1\u63a7\u6307\u6807\u4f53\u7cfb\n\n**RAG\u7cfb\u7edfSLO\u793a\u4f8b\uff1a**\n\n| SLI | SLO\u76ee\u6807 | \u6d4b\u91cf\u5468\u671f |\n|-----|--------|---------|\n| \u53ef\u7528\u6027 | 99.9% | \u6eda\u52a830\u5929 |\n| P95\u5ef6\u8fdf | <1000ms | \u6eda\u52a87\u5929 |\n| Faithfulness | >95% | \u6eda\u52a87\u5929 |\n| \u9519\u8bef\u7387 | <1% | \u6eda\u52a830\u5929 |\n\n**Error Budget\u71c3\u70e7\u7387\u544a\u8b66\uff1a**\n\n```python\nslo_target = 0.999  # 99.9%\nerror_budget = 1.0 - slo_target  # 0.1%\n\ncurrent_error_rate = 1.0 - availability_sli\nburn_rate = current_error_rate / error_budget\n\n# \u544a\u8b66\u9608\u503c\uff08Google SRE\u63a8\u8350\uff09\nif burn_rate > 14.4:  # 2\u5c0f\u65f6\u8017\u5c3d\u6708\u5ea6\u9884\u7b97\n    trigger_critical_alert()\nelif burn_rate > 6:  # 5\u5c0f\u65f6\u8017\u5c3d\u6708\u5ea6\u9884\u7b97\n    trigger_high_alert()\n```\n\n**RAG\u56db\u9ec4\u91d1\u4fe1\u53f7\uff1a**\n1. **\u5ef6\u8fdf**: GPU\u5185\u5b58\u5229\u7528\u7387\u3001KV\u7f13\u5b58\u5360\u7528\u3001\u5411\u91cf\u5e93\u67e5\u8be2\u961f\u5217\u6df1\u5ea6\n\n**PromQL\u793a\u4f8b\uff1a**\n\n```promql\n# \u8bf7\u6c42\u7387\nrate(rag_requests_total[5m])\n\n# \u9519\u8bef\u7387\nsum(rate(rag_requests_total{status=\"error\"}[5m])) \n  / sum(rate(rag_requests_total[5m]))\n\n# P95\u5ef6\u8fdf\nhistogram_quantile(0.95, rate(rag_request_duration_seconds_bucket[5m]))\n\n# \u5e73\u5747TTFT\nrate(rag_ttft_seconds_sum[5m]) / rate(rag_ttft_seconds_count[5m])\n\n# Error Budget\u71c3\u70e7\u7387\n(1 - (sum(rate(rag_requests_total{status=\"success\"}[1h])) \n  / sum(rate(rag_requests_total[1h])))) / 0.001  # 99.9% SLO\n```\n\n**Grafana
